## 0. Targets and conventions

- Target 1. L-001 (Theorem 2w) (THEOREM2_PROOF_v4 §1.3): for odd L > 11, SU(2), d = 4, there are γ, g_1, β, B′, D′ such that for every K ≥ 1 and g ∈ ]0, g_1] a bare coupling g_0(K, g) gives g_k ∈ ]0, γ], g_K = g, the two-sided window bound (c′), and K-018 (S). Its use: discharge the coupling hypothesis of CMP 122 II Theorem 1.
- Target 2. Finite-torus existence in compactness form (ASSEMBLY §1.3; paper L-003 (Theorem B)): subsequence limits of the lattice Schwinger functions S_{n,ε} on D_n exist, and every limit satisfies (OS0)–(OS3) on T⁴_ℓ. Hypothesis H-116 (H-U) is the output of B-T2 (= Target 1 + Theorem 1), B-S (sourced Theorem 1), and the bridge B-N1.
- Scope rule. A row appears if Target 1 or Target 2 uses it and the repository has no proof that a verifier confirmed. Items that are proved and verified here are not rows; §6 lists the ones that left the ledger in this campaign.
- Status words. "asserted (Balaban)": Balaban prints the statement without proof, or with "we can prove", "similarly", "not repeated", or "announced". "proved (Balaban)": Balaban gives a proof in print; cite. "implicit": Balaban uses the statement without printing it; we found the use. "[R]": our reduction to named inputs, verified unless marked. "[I]": an inference step of ours that no verifier proved. "numerical": measured or certified only at points. "open": no proof, no reduction.
- Paper short names: CMP 95, 96, 98, 99 (1984–85 propagators, averaging, background fields); CMP 102 (1985, minimizers, Prop. 9); CMP 109 (1987, RG I, Theorem 2 announced); CMP 116 (1988, RG II); CMP 119 (1988, RG III); CMP 122 I, II (1989, RG IV).

## 1. Group A — Balaban-level assertions taken on trust

Each [R]/[I] item of ours that bottoms out in a Balaban statement is traced in the "depends" column. R-001–R-010 and R-012–R-015 are statements Balaban prints (R-047 and R-048 are now in §6) (R-015 moved from implicit to printed, TRUST_LEDGER_VERIFY Step 12; R-002 is the walk-expansion form of R-001, CMP 99 p. 422 "analyzed in the same way" and Thm 3.15 p. 432). R-016–R-019 are statements he uses without printing them (found by us); R-050 left the ledger in round 4 (§6). R-020 records one thing he does not print (the large-field T-step rate loss). R-049 and R-051 are in §6. R-022, R-023 (round 10) are printed CMP 109 assertions (p. 253, p. 275) that earlier results transfer to the new averaging and the new backgrounds. R-024 (round 11, Q-049) is the printed use of CMP 99 Thms 3.7–3.10 "in their full generality" in the multiscale Ω-geometry (CMP 119 pp. 275–276).
